As part of the transaction, Vodafone has received &#8364;1.0 billion in cash and a 10% equity interest in Ziggo Group, which will hold Liberty Global's interests in VodafoneZiggo in the Netherlands and Telenet in Belgium and Luxembourg. Liberty Global holds the remaining 90% of Ziggo Group. Liberty Global also provided an update regarding its previously announced intention to pursue a transaction pursuant to which Liberty Global would distribute to its shareholders its entire equity interest in Ziggo Group, a holding company that owns, directly or indirectly, all of the equity interests in Telenet and VodafoneZiggo Group Holding B.V., and seek to list the shares of Ziggo Group on Euronext Amsterdam (the &#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Spin Transaction</span><span <title>Document</title></head><body><div id="i7bfa82548d6e4f0c9c081b89fa761d04_1"></div><div style="min-height:223.2pt;width:100%"><div style="margin-top:1pt;text-align:right"><table style="border-collapse:collapse;display:inline-table;margin-bottom:5pt;vertical-align:text-bottom;width:100.000%"><tr><td style="width:1.0%"></td><td style="width:98.900%"></td><td style="width:0.1%"></td></tr><tr style="height:185pt"><td colspan="3" style="padding:0 1pt;vertical-align:bottom"><div style="margin-bottom:8pt;text-align:center"><img alt="pressreleasev1a.jpg" src="pressreleasev1a.jpg" style="height:224px;margin-bottom:5pt;vertical-align:text-bottom;width:664px"></div></td></tr></table></div></div><div style="margin-bottom:8pt;text-align:right"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:115%">Exhibit 99.1</font></div><div><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:20pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">LIBERTY GLOBAL COMPLETES BUYOUT OF VODAFONEZIGGO AS IT PREPARES FOR 2027 ZIGGO GROUP LISTING</font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:139%">Denver, Colorado&#59; London, UK</font><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:139%"> &#8211; August 03, 2026</font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">Liberty Global Ltd. (NASDAQ&#58; LBTYA, LBTYB and LBTYK) today announced the completion of its acquisition of Vodafone Group Plc's 50% shareholding in VodafoneZiggo, paving the way for the creation of Ziggo Group, the Benelux connectivity champion with 13 million customers and &#8364;6.6bn of revenue*. </font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">As part of the transaction, Vodafone has received approximately &#8364;1.0 billion in cash and a 10% equity interest in Ziggo Group, which will hold Liberty Global's interests in VodafoneZiggo in the Netherlands and Telenet in Belgium and Luxembourg. Liberty Global holds the remaining 90% of Ziggo Group. </font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">The completion of the transaction marks a significant milestone in Liberty Global's strategy to unlock value in its telecommunications portfolio. As previously announced, the company plans to list Ziggo Group in Amsterdam in 2027 by spinning-off the 90% held by Liberty Global to its shareholders. The proposed spin-off is intended to be tax free for US shareholders of Liberty Global (with evaluation of tax treatment in other jurisdictions ongoing), and follows the successful spin-off of the company's Swiss telecoms group, Sunrise, which has delivered significant returns for shareholders. </font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font><br></font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">Plans for the listing of Ziggo Group are already under way with VodafoneZiggo CEO Stephen van Rooyen appointed CEO of Ziggo Group and Sunrise CFO Jany Fruytier becoming CFO once Ziggo Group begins operations in September. </font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font><br></font></div><div><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">The financial separation of Telenet&#8217;s and Wyre&#8217;s credit facilities has also been completed following the Belgian Competition Authority&#8217;s approval of the network collaboration in Flanders between Wyre and Proximus. </font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">Wyre has drawn &#8364;2.71bn ($3.13bn) of debt from its &#8364;4.35bn ($5.02bn) bank facility. The use of proceeds included a &#8364;398m ($460m) dividend to Telenet and the repayment of a &#8364;1.98bn ($2.28bn) intercompany loan to Telenet. Telenet then used proceeds to repay &#8364;2.12bn ($2.45bn) of its own debt, maturing in 2028. </font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">In addition, &#8364;1.2-1.4bn ($1.4-1.6bn) of asset disposals across the Ziggo Group (50% of Telenet&#8217;s stake in Wyre, VodafoneZiggo&#8217;s tower portfolio and certain property assets in Belgium and Holland) are under way with the proceeds being used to retire debt. </font></div><div style="height:20.88pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div><font><br></font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:223.2pt;width:100%"><div style="margin-top:1pt;text-align:right"><table style="border-collapse:collapse;display:inline-table;margin-bottom:5pt;vertical-align:text-bottom;width:100.000%"><tr><td style="width:1.0%"></td><td style="width:98.900%"></td><td style="width:0.1%"></td></tr><tr style="height:185pt"><td colspan="3" style="padding:0 1pt;vertical-align:bottom"><div style="margin-bottom:8pt;text-align:center"><img alt="pressreleasev1a.jpg" src="pressreleasev1a.jpg" style="height:224px;margin-bottom:5pt;vertical-align:text-bottom;width:664px"></div></td></tr></table></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:140%">Mike Fries, Liberty Global Chairman and CEO, said</font><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">&#58; &#8220;Ziggo Group is already the most important telecommunications company in the Benelux region, with the scale to deliver the highest quality services to residential and enterprise customers and the ambition to create long-term value for shareholders. Local investors will soon have the opportunity to invest in a regional champion with strong customer propositions and a compelling outlook for free cash flow generation and dividends over time. </font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font><br></font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">&#8220;I&#8217;m also delighted that Vodafone will remain a 10% shareholder in the Ziggo Group. They have been an outstanding partner for nearly a decade and we will always maintain a strong relationship with Margherita and her team.&#8221; </font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font><br></font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:140%">Stephen van Rooyen, VodafoneZiggo CEO and intended Ziggo Group CEO said&#58; </font><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">&#8220;Today marks the start of Ziggo Group. For customers, nothing changes&#58; they keep the same trusted brands they know today. Behind the scenes, though, we're creating a stronger company with greater ability to invest, innovate and build for the future. Our ambition is simple&#58; combine the strength of a larger group with the focus and entrepreneurial spirit of strong local businesses.&#8221; </font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font><br></font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">* Financial data as of December 31, 2025 and represents the combined results of the VodafoneZiggo JV and Telenet, excluding Wyre. US GAAP and IFRS are broadly similar. For more additional information please see the Liberty Global Q1 2026 investor presentation.</font></div><div style="margin-top:15pt"><font style="color:#ee742b;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:12pt;font-weight:700;line-height:139%">ABOUT LIBERTY GLOBAL </font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:162%">Liberty Global Ltd.</font><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:162%"> (Nasdaq&#58; LBTYA, LBTYB, LBTYK) delivers long-term shareholder value through the strategic management of two complementary platforms&#58; Liberty Telecom and Liberty Growth.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font><br></font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:139%">Liberty Telecom</font><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:139%"> is a world leader in converged broadband, video and mobile communications, providing approximately 80&#160;million fixed and mobile connections across Europe through advanced fiber and 5G networks that empower customers and strengthen national economies. The business generates aggregate revenue of $22&#160;billion, including approximately $18&#160;billion from nonconsolidated joint ventures and $4&#160;billion from consolidated operations.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font><br></font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:139%">Liberty Growth</font><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:139%"> invests in scalable businesses across the technology, media, sports and infrastructure sectors, with a portfolio of roughly 70 companies and funds valued at $3.4&#160;billion.*</font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font><br></font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font style="color:#243347;font-family:'Arial',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:162%">Together, these platforms reflect Liberty Global's focus on operating, enabling and investing in businesses with strong strategic fit and the potential to deliver sustainable long-term returns.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font><br></font></div><div><font
